- **response-to-reviewers**: Drafts the Response-to-Reviewers document and fills the R&R Traceability Matrix. Reads _review/round-&lt;N&gt;/reviewer-comments.md + response-matrix.json (built by sws_response_matrix.py first) + the paper drafts. For each reviewer comment, scores soundness 1-5; concedes only when score &gt;= 4 AND the requested change does not contradict the manuscript's thesis or evidence (D12). "evidence 검증 결과", "diagnosis", "verification outcome", "답변 수정 제안", "HTML 리포트" 관련 요청 시 호출된다. ([youai058/research_hub](https://github.com/youai058/research_hub/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/results-analyst.md))
- **results-analyzer**: Analyze training results.csv for convergence, overfitting, and loss dynamics. ([MacroMan5/autotrain-yolo](https://github.com/MacroMan5/autotrain-yolo/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/results-analyzer.md) · ★9)
- **results-analyzer**: Analyzes k6 NDJSON result files in results/ — computes p95/p99, error rates, per-scenario trends, and compares the latest run against previous runs to flag regressions. Use after local load tests, or when the user asks how a run compares to earlier ones. ([hoanglk12/k6-ecommerce-framework](https://github.com/hoanglk12/k6-ecommerce-framework/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/results-analyzer.md))
- **results-analyzer**: Parses a Kaggle training log (pasted text or file) and reports the metric pack, val-test gap, Δ-vs-paper, and a tracker-ready row. Use PROACTIVELY when the user pastes a log, says "here's the log", or runs /analyze-log. - **results-auditor**: Audits the results or analysis section of a WNE UW licencjat thesis — checks separation of findings from interpretation, direct address of research question, and scope-claim alignment. - **results-critic**: Reads one results table against the pre-registered stop rule in a fresh context and returns survived, killed or inconclusive, quoting the clause that decided it. Never sees who ran the experiment, what they hoped for, or anything the tabulator wrote about how it went. Dispatched by the result skill, once per experiment. - **RESULTS-harden**: Claude Code headless, Sonnet. - **results-reader**: Where the platform exposes per-cell delivered demographics, run t-tests of mean gender and age across cells before grading any comparison.
